<h2>Model Support</h2>
<ul>
<li><strong>New models</strong>: DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56214">#56214</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56228">#56228</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56208">#56208</a>), DeepSeek-V4-Flash-Vision-Exp (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54566">#54566</a>) with ROCm support (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55107">#55107</a>) and LoRA (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55897">#55897</a>), GLM-5.3-Flash (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/53906">#53906</a>), K2-Horizon with its reasoning and tool parsers (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55063">#55063</a>), Cohere Compass (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54774">#54774</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55949">#55949</a>), Bailing V3 VL with MTP (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55921">#55921</a>), Nanbeige4.2 via the Transformers backend (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56071">#56071</a>), and a DeepSeek-V4 CPU backend (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55355">#55355</a>).</li>
<li><strong>DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash</strong>: whole KV in MXFP8 through the FlashMLA V4.1 record on SM100 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56893">#56893</a>), Mega-mHC from the DeepGEMM fork (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56962">#56962</a>), mHC post block folded into the delayed pre projection (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56633">#56633</a>), Triton-fused input metadata preparation (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56562">#56562</a>), async prefetch for CPU-offloaded Engram lookups and Engram DP sharding (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56512">#56512</a>), DSpark draft states collapsed before the SP all-gather (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56903">#56903</a>), DSpark drafter no longer inheriting uninitialized EPLB state (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56387">#56387</a>), XGrammar V4.1 schema constraints for strict tool parameters (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56408">#56408</a>), Responses text parts (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56299">#56299</a>), and image sentinel padding scoped to the Vision-Exp checkpoint (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56554">#56554</a>).</li>
<li><strong>DeepSeek V4</strong>: plain RoPE without YaRN on sparse SWA layers (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54815">#54815</a>), <code>--kv-cache-dtype auto</code> resolving to <code>fp8_ds_mla</code> with FlashMLA (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/45091">#45091</a>), prefill sparse index workspace sentinel seeded (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55299">#55299</a>), dequant gather grid sized by rows (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55061">#55061</a>), optional Q-norm in the fused MLA epilogue and group_size=32 packed FP8 quant (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56215">#56215</a>), sparse settings read from the text config for composite models (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56160">#56160</a>), MegaMoE startup without EP fixed (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55914">#55914</a>), and a Triton iHC pre/post fallback for HY V4 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55059">#55059</a>).</li>
<li><strong>GLM-5.3-Flash</strong>: EPLB support (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55119">#55119</a>), FlashKDA for KDA chunked prefill (1.7-3.8x faster than the Triton chunk path, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55737">#55737</a>), dense and masked-MHA sparse prefill for the NoPE (256, 0, 256) layout (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55738">#55738</a>), decode hot-path cleanups removing per-layer copies and a duplicate router GEMM (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55736">#55736</a>), and the <code>glm5next</code> NVIDIA subtree now packaged in wheels (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55214">#55214</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Qwen3.8-Flash-Next</strong>: separate prefill and decode QSA indexer kernels with <code>VLLM_SPARSE_INDEXER_MAX_LOGITS_MB</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54513">#54513</a>), fused PLE kernels and merged K/V projections (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54517">#54517</a>), sparse GQA skipping padded indices (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54873">#54873</a>), FP8 indexer cache (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54890">#54890</a>), fused PLE residual and QSA output gate (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55309">#55309</a>), UVA PLE offload and Engram tensor parallelism via <code>--engram-config</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54371">#54371</a>), torch.compile removed from the NVIDIA path (<a class="issue-link href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55890">#55890</a>), and fixes for FP8 PLE weight scales (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54722">#54722</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54882">#54882</a>) and fused PLE conv strides (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55375">#55375</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Kimi K3</strong>: native CUDA AttnRes default on SM100 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54261">#54261</a>), ll_bf16 router weight prefetch (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/53524">#53524</a>), overlapped TP8 KDA projections (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54697">#54697</a>), strided DSV3 GEMM (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54565">#54565</a>), MLA decode epilogue latency (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54896">#54896</a>), NVFP4 in_proj alignment routing to DeepGEMM (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55242">#55242</a>), mixed-batch KDA without gather/scatter (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56159">#56159</a>), grouped FP8 MLA cache insertion (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55356">#55356</a>), FlashInfer KDA kernels (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55364">#55364</a>), FlashKDA bf16 checkpoint state (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56485">#56485</a>) and a stability bump (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54859">#54859</a>), SiTU on the FlashInfer CuTeDSL MoE backend (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54606">#54606</a>), internal prefix checkpoints with partial prefix caching and spec decoding (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54634">#54634</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55774">#55774</a>), GDN metadata (<a href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54781">#54781</a>), checkpoint indexing (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55747">#55747</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56794">#56794</a>), KDA illegal memory access (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55426">#55426</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Transformers backend</strong>: attention found with a fuser and vLLM's layer attached to it (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54941">#54941</a>), generic merged-column linear fusion (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55301">#55301</a>), QKV fuser for Gemma4 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55690">#55690</a>), all vocab embeddings replaced including Gemma 3n/4 per-layer tables (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54760">#54760</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55083">#55083</a>), and attention sinks applied (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/52156">#52156</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Speculative decoding for models</strong>: EAGLE3 for Sarvam MLA (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/53052">#53052</a>), Nemotron-H MTP with a separate, possibly quantized lm_head (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54574">#54574</a>), NVFP4 DSpark gathered top-k projection for quantized Markov heads (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55713">#55713</a>), Qwen3.5/3.6 multimodal MTP resolving <code>n_predict</code> from the text config (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55369">#55369</a>), Qwen3 DSpark padded-vocab drafts (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55133">#55133</a>), and GLM-OCR MTP weight loading (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49869">#49869</a>) and position masking under CUDA graphs (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56447">#56447</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Multimodal</strong>: torchcodec audio decoding and a selectable <code>audio_backend</code> in <code>--media-io-kwargs</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/51826">#51826</a>) with automatic decoding kept soundfile-first (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55642">#55642</a>), torchaudio as the default resampler (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/52598">#52598</a>), <code>media_io_kwargs</code> in multimodal hashes (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54241">#54241</a>), cache hash kwargs scoped by modality (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54918">#54918</a>), empty video URLs with multimodal UUIDs (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54220">#54220</a>), cached audio inputs with UUIDs (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56310">#56310</a>), encoder CUDA graphs for MiniCPM-V 2.5/2.6/4.0 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/42785">#42785</a>), Voxtral Realtime with <code>FULL_DECODE_ONLY</code> graphs (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/51167">#51167</a>), Triton/FlashInfer composite attention for multimodal prefixes (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56305">#56305</a>), pruned sliding-window tiles for Gemma 4 multimodal prompts (up to 3-4x E2E, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/53147">#53147</a>), SDPA for BLIP-2 Q-Former (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55285">#55285</a>), baddbmm Conformer scores (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55062">#55062</a>), fused DeepEncoder relative bias (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55629">#55629</a>), Gemma3n sparse GELU Triton kernel (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48498">#48498</a>), and no duplicate text embedding in Qwen2.5-Omni (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55415">#55415</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Correctness</strong>: Gemma 4 / Gemma 3n fine-tuned checkpoints without KV projections on shared layers (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54917">#54917</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55864">#55864</a>), Gemma4 video preprocessing without image kwargs (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56652">#56652</a>) and unified audio profiling (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56721">#56721</a>), MiniCPM-V 4.6 loading (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49417">#49417</a>) and video embeds (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54633">#54633</a>), InternVL video parser (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55779">#55779</a>), Nano-Nemotron precomputed embeddings (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56398">#56398</a>), OpenPangu multimodal merge (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55941">#55941</a>) and sleep mode (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/53696">#53696</a>), Qwen3-VL and Cosmos3-Edge under CPU and PP (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/53699">#53699</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/43272">#43272</a>), Qwen VL processors without a tokenizer (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54886">#54886</a>), Molmo2 fps guard (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55893">#55893</a>), CohereASR audio-token estimate (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/53829">#53829</a>), double BOS in <code>LLM.chat()</code> for multimodal models (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55288">#55288</a>), decoded frame lists parsed as one video (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55326">#55326</a>), swapped H/W in dummy video profiling (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56385">#56385</a>), base64 video validation (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54323">#54323</a>), Sentence Transformers 5.4+ configs (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54533">#54533</a>), ColQwen3.5 pooler projector (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54847">#54847</a>), torch.compile enabled for StableLM (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54969">#54969</a>) and Sarvam MLA (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55817">#55817</a>), and Nemotron latent-MoE skipping a redundant all-reduce at TP&gt;1 (about 13% decode win, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/52301">#52301</a>).</li>
<li><strong>LoRA</strong>: rsLoRA scaling in MoE expert packing (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55548">#55548</a>), a log line when an adapter applies no weights (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55310">#55310</a>), and <code>--lora-modules name=path</code> with <code>=</code> in the path (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/53353">#53353</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h2>Engine Core</h2>
<ul>
<li><strong>Model Runner V2</strong>: DBO in eager mode (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50945">#50945</a>) and with FULL CUDA graphs (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/51700">#51700</a>), MTP (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46994">#46994</a>) and EAGLE3-style drafts (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50514">#50514</a>) under pipeline parallelism, adaptive verification for all draft-model speculators (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/52228">#52228</a>), gc frozen during capture (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54646">#54646</a>), GPU-compacted sampling masks (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54901">#54901</a>), UVA-backed <code>apply_write</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55819">#55819</a>), fast-prefill support (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56145">#56145</a>), DP sync skipped for all speculator uniform decodes (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54856">#54856</a>), prompt embeddings in decoder pooling (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56639">#56639</a>), pooling post-processing on non-final PP ranks (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56666">#56666</a>), kernels warmed before graph capture (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55341">#55341</a>), <code>CUDAGraphStat</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/52358">#52358</a>), Proton CUDA graph attribution (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/51084">#51084</a>), platforms without pinned memory such as WSL (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56908">#56908</a>), draft <code>moe_backend</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54788">#54788</a>) and <code>attention_backend</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54826">#54826</a>) overrides honored, DCP metadata initialized after batch partitioning (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55212">#55212</a>), and a <code>record_stream</code> fix in the PP draft broadcast (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55745">#55745</a>). The MRV2-specific Triton JIT warmup migration was reverted (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56654">#56654</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Speculative decoding</strong>: opt-in <code>disable_eagle_block_drop</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/53388">#53388</a>), adaptive verification on <code>FLASHINFER_MLA_SPARSE_DSV4</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/52724">#52724</a>) and deferred until after kernel warmup (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55455">#55455</a>), full-vocab EAGLE3 drafters skipping vocab remapping (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/53458">#53458</a>), DSpark preserving the target DCP config (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55472">#55472</a>), DFlash drafters dropping FlashAttention's AOT schedule (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54374">#54374</a>), DP token padding excluded from draft metadata (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55458">#55458</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56181">#56181</a>), fastsafetensors deadlock avoided for PP drafts (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54416">#54416</a>), Mamba state cached at the EAGLE resume position (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/53945">#53945</a>) and preserved for padded prompt tails (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55178">#55178</a>), resumed requests padded in P/D (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55126">#55126</a>), and warmup device selection (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55245">#55245</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Prefix caching and scheduling</strong>: both replay boundaries retained so an EAGLE resend of a block-aligned prompt still hits (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54713">#54713</a>), zero-progress preemption cascades stopped for deferred KV frees (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49675">#49675</a>), SWA layers taking the primary block size to avoid inflating the KV block LCM (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/53007">#53007</a>), align-mode Mamba states retired across null gaps (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55450">#55450</a>), the <code>all</code> Mamba cache mode deprecated with MRV1 fallback (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55041">#55041</a>), and <code>vllm:request_num_preemptions</code> histogram (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49984">#49984</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Kernels</strong>: FlashInfer ReplaySSM backend for Mamba2 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/52506">#52506</a>), B12X causal paged attention for SM120/SM121 via <code>--attention-backend B12X_ATTN</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/52017">#52017</a>), FlashInfer XQA decode on SM90 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50439">#50439</a>), DeepSelect top-k with <code>--sparse-indexer-topk-backend</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56464">#56464</a>), BF16x3 router GEMM default on SM100 for FP32-router models (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55899">#55899</a>), split-row Triton top-p for small batches (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54651">#54651</a>), manual <code>ActivationQuantFusionPass</code> producer for Llama (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/51415">#51415</a>), fused add-RMSNorm + NVFP4 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/51925">#51925</a>), prefix-prefill tiling independent of KV page size (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54194">#54194</a>), FlashInfer GDN prefill on SM12x (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55715">#55715</a>), fused GDN MTP decode for SM110 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/53835">#53835</a>), masked MoE activation for padded layouts (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/51217">#51217</a>), cooperative <code>batched_moe_align_block_size</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/53280">#53280</a>), PDL in <code>fusedQKNormRopeKernel</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55755">#55755</a>) and weight prefetch in fused q/kv RMSNorm (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55020">#55020</a>), XD-RoPE folded into M-RoPE (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56078">#56078</a>), and the FlashAttention pin synced with upstream (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54819">#54819</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Startup and warmup</strong>: renderer warmup overlapped with engine core init (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54557">#54557</a>), FlashInfer autotune no longer rerun on each source change (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54794">#54794</a>), Qwen3.5/Qwen3-Next Triton kernels (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54797">#54797</a>) and GDN gated RMSNorm (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54251">#54251</a>) warmed at startup, Gemma 4 de-JITified (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55768">#55768</a>), DeepSeek V4 and shared attention, sampling, DFlash and mHC kernels migrated to the JIT warmup registry (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50175">#50175</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/53564">#53564</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/53565">#53565</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50176">#50176</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/53566">#53566</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56323">#56323</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50178">#50178</a>) with parallel mHC pre-norm compilation (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56683">#56683</a>), FlashInfer deferred MoE decode kernels autotuned before capture (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55377">#55377</a>), and supplemental BF16 autotuning isolated (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57285">#57285</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Fast Start</strong>: weight-cache daemon with <code>--load-format ipc_cache</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54921">#54921</a>), FP4 checkpoints (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55465">#55465</a>), multi-node TP (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55468">#55468</a>), GPU UUID socket folders (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56669">#56669</a>), per-client IPC tensor export so a copy-mode client cannot release zero-copy weights (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56472">#56472</a>), and a clear error on non-CUDA/ROCm platforms (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56010">#56010</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Watermarking</strong>: Gumbel-max watermarked generation and detection (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54053">#54053</a>) and dual-key Gumbel-max for speculative decoding with <code>allow_target_only_watermarking</code> and context deduplication (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56122">#56122</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Sleep and RL</strong>: NCCL communicator memory released in sleep mode (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/51485">#51485</a>), DP state synced on the first step of a wave so pause and sleep stop burning 32 dummy forwards per rank (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/52957">#52957</a>), pre-shared <code>ncclUniqueId</code> rendezvous for torch-free trainers (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/53784">#53784</a>), stale HPC QK-norm weights after refit fixed (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56706">#56706</a>), and <code>session_id</code> on GPU <code>BlockStored</code> events (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/51381">#51381</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Robustness</strong>: attention implementations must explicitly declare DCP support (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55780">#55780</a>), unavailable piecewise CUDA graphs raise instead of producing garbled output (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54782">#54782</a>), non-compiled models fall back to <code>FULL_DECODE_ONLY</code> graphs (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55095">#55095</a>), MRV1 breakable graphs scoped to the piecewise path (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56312">#56312</a>), sync checks cover paged async copies (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/53491">#53491</a>) and torch.compile (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56904">#56904</a>), more H2D copies pinned (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54660">#54660</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55202">#55202</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56138">#56138</a>), GDN capture metadata built without a device sync (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55404">#55404</a>), queued work carried over when materializing the dedicated stream (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56382">#56382</a>), FlexAttention recompiles avoided (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55888">#55888</a>), persistent top-k falling back on low-shared-memory GPUs (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54110">#54110</a>), KV page size scaled for hidden-state extraction with TP (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50894">#50894</a>), out-of-bounds access in SM90 sparse MLA mixed batches (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56969">#56969</a>), sparse-MLA piecewise capture crash (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56825">#56825</a>), TRT-LLM ragged prefill regression (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55499">#55499</a>), MoE fused sum int32 overflow (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50220">#50220</a>), odd-row per-token-group quant cliff (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56478">#56478</a>), dummy-load re-initialization (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56682">#56682</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56688">#56688</a>), <code>warning_once</code> retaining the LLM instance (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54098">#54098</a>), DSpark cache-group capability under <code>python -O</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55234">#55234</a>), sparse-MLA DCP stabilized for GLM PCP configurations (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55879">#55879</a>), DeepGEMM FP8 warmup coverage (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56452">#56452</a>), Triton autotune inspection skipped without Triton (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56676">#56676</a>), <code>mm_device_do_normalize</code> under encoder CUDA graphs (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55370">#55370</a>), VidCom2 frame budgets read once (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55331">#55331</a>), the CUDA profiler start fix (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55237">#55237</a>), FlashInfer all-reduce workspace failures logged as errors (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55127">#55127</a>), and backend-neutral KV cache and MFU log lines (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55650">#55650</a>).</li>
</ul>

<h2>Large Scale Serving</h2>
<ul>
<li><strong>Context parallelism</strong>: PCP+DCP on sparse-MLA models (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56157">#56157</a>), PCP with single-module MTP and replicated DSpark (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56107">#56107</a>), PCP decode-only FULL CUDA graphs (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/53867">#53867</a>), FlashMLASparse MTP at CP interleave &gt; 1 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56722">#56722</a>), symmetric DCP disaggregation for hybrid Mamba models (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55531">#55531</a>), DCP empty-shard LSE mask fused into the A2A pack kernel (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54889">#54889</a>), DCP prefill keys materialized on non-owner ranks (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54908">#54908</a>), DCP indexer interleave guard removed (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55802">#55802</a>) and respected in the PCP gather (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56715">#56715</a>), PCP producer shards as NIXL transfer ranks (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56645">#56645</a>), replicated-PCP ranks reporting done (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/53903">#53903</a>), the DCP interleave adjustment scoped to NixlConnector (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54803">#54803</a>), PCP counted in multi-node world size (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55111">#55111</a>), PCP-DP validation moved to the GPU manager (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54523">#54523</a>), and one-sided EP at DP=1 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54016">#54016</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Elastic EP and MoE communication</strong>: CUDA graphs reused across reconfiguration (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54985">#54985</a>), routing only to surviving engines on scale-down (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55772">#55772</a>), DeepEP v2 async finalize (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/52781">#52781</a>), FlashInfer PCIe IPC all-reduce (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/53576">#53576</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54869">#54869</a>), batched CUTLASS workspace sizing (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55579">#55579</a>), and PP never dropping a decoding request from the sampled-token broadcast (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54436">#54436</a>).</li>
<li><strong>KV connectors</strong>: Mooncake Store heterogeneous TP sharing (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/53129">#53129</a>), heterogeneous PP completion (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56033">#56033</a>), replicated GQA heads (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/52516">#52516</a>), physical-block transfer length (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54272">#54272</a>), producer partial Mamba tails (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/52832">#52832</a>), hybrid-model finish-time saves (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54643">#54643</a>), failed remote loads reported to the scheduler (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50984">#50984</a>), QSA ring groups excluded (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55027">#55027</a>); NIXL per-region transfer geometry (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/53780">#53780</a>), int32 indices (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/51952">#51952</a>), multi-handle race (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56104">#56104</a>), no eviction mid-transfer (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54689">#54689</a>), full prefix hits reported finished (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56640">#56640</a>), notification-only requests (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57570">#57570</a>), and double cleanup (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54518">#54518</a>); DecodeBench HMA, DCP, prefix and circular-buffer fixes (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54647">#54647</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54679">#54679</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54878">#54878</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54879">#54879</a>); connector block tables resolved for every scheduled request (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54853">#54853</a>), hybrid KV load failures (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50388">#50388</a>), and <code>lmcache_mp_connector</code> state transitions guarded (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47505">#47505</a>); NixlPush remote-agent reads guarded (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56317">#56317</a>).</li>
<li><strong>KV offloading</strong>: KVCR secondary-tier adapter (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/53624">#53624</a>), P2P tier <code>unbound_store_timeout_s</code> and late-fetch rejection (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/53453">#53453</a>), OffloadingConnector retention interval (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/51886">#51886</a>) and offload hits under MTP/EAGLE (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/52771">#52771</a>), SimpleCPUOffload fine-grained hybrid prefix hits (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54736">#54736</a>), eager registration and final flush (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/53532">#53532</a>), <code>BlockStored</code> metadata (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54325">#54325</a>), mixed page sizes for DSA models (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54756">#54756</a>), prefix-cache bypass (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54998">#54998</a>), no-forward-step stores (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56621">#56621</a>); UniformTypeKVCacheSpecs portability (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/51690">#51690</a>) and DCP scaling (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50883">#50883</a>); and fixes for async lookups (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54872">#54872</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55075">#55075</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55823">#55823</a>), SWA reachability and coverage (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54362">#54362</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55712">#55712</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56486">#56486</a>), final-token slot (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54288">#54288</a>), oversized offers (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54759">#54759</a>), ARC fallback (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55461">#55461</a>), disk alignment (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55424">#55424</a>), DiskBackend buffer race (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/51667">#51667</a>), offload key ordering (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/52923">#52923</a>), prefetch slot ownership (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54975">#54975</a>), load boundaries (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/52807">#52807</a>), and tiering shutdown (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/52290">#52290</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Encoder cache and EPD</strong>: P2P NIXL + CPU EC connector (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/47941">#47941</a>), ECMooncakeConnector (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/41567">#41567</a>), embedding inputs auto-enabled on EC/KV consumers (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56379">#56379</a>), GPU NVDEC for encoder-only instances (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/53675">#53675</a>), lightweight multimodal metadata from render (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54659">#54659</a>) as JSON arrays (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56090">#56090</a>), reduced proxy serialization overhead (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56657">#56657</a>), media options and UUIDs preserved (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56786">#56786</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56432">#56432</a>), per-item EC checks (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56070">#56070</a>), ECCPUWorker backend extension points (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54033">#54033</a>), and fixes for old kernels (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/53190">#53190</a>), remote encoding failures (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55290">#55290</a>), and TP&gt;1 load devices (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/40416">#40416</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h2>Quantization</h2>
<ul>
<li><strong>New capabilities</strong>: targeted online quantization (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/51285">#51285</a>) and online quantization of partially pre-quantized checkpoints (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/51392">#51392</a>), W4A16 DSA (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/51724">#51724</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55538">#55538</a>), FlashInfer CuTeDSL NVFP4 W4A16 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/53014">#53014</a>), NVFP4 torch backend (#53319), <code>linear_backend_per_quant</code> (#51204), AutoRound 2/3/5/6/7-bit (#52890), TRT-LLM FP8 block-scale MoE with SwiGLU clamp (#55069), and Quark per-block FP8 MoE on ROCm (#52263).</li>
<li><strong>Refactors</strong>: Quark dispatch on <code>QuantKey</code> (#52958) and ModelOpt linear methods unified into one QuantKey-driven method (#49381).</li>
<li><strong>Fixes</strong>: NVFP4 padding initialized in-kernel (#53568), unloaded NVFP4 scales detected with a NaN sentinel (#52501), NVFP4 fused SiLU+mul scales (#55643), Quark per-block scales registered as <code>weight_scale</code> (#54770), GPT-OSS FP8 MoE with Quark (#54824), FSE detection for Quark models (#54573), DSv4 MXFP4 backend alias selection (#53586), unset <code>group_size</code> on compressed-tensors WNA16 MoE (#53163), block FP8 MTP in ModelOpt mixed checkpoints (#55513), and unquantized MoE weight storage preserved on ROCm for RL updates (#46009).</li>
</ul>
<h2>API &amp; Frontend</h2>
<ul>
<li><strong>New endpoints and options</strong>: stateless <code>/v1/responses/render</code> (#50195), scale-out endpoints behind <code>--enable-scale-out</code> (#54579, #55176), <code>count_reasoning_tokens</code> so usage reports <code>reasoning_tokens</code> (#54982), prompt metadata from <code>/inference/v1/generate</code> (#53187), <code>vllm:request_num_preemptions</code> (#49984), MFU/MBU for SWA and hybrid layers (#55624), consolidated Prometheus bucket defaults (#48866), site-packages reasoning and tool parser plugins (#45241), and <code>max_num_queued_reqs</code> shared across API server processes (#54746).</li>
<li><strong>Structured output and parsers</strong>: XGrammar <code>patternProperties</code>/<code>propertyNames</code>/<code>unevaluatedProperties</code> (#42904), full-history reasoning scans eliminated (#55223) with <code>is_reasoning_end</code> derived from the grammar (#56200), invalid structured-output requests no longer stopping the engine (#51450), a unified Cohere Command parser (#56392), MCP SDK 2.x schemas (#53870), incomplete tool-call markup dropped consistently (#47562), DSML tool calls without the wrapper (#55954), misspelled wrappers (#56141) and implicit parameter closes (#54838), Gemma4 malformed openers (#53444), Seed-OSS turn boundaries (#54264), Step-3.5 with structured outputs (#53174), reasoning parser plugins loaded before headless config (#53124), and a warning for removed <code>guided_*</code> fields (#54285).</li>
<li><strong>OpenAI, Anthropic and Cohere compatibility</strong>: unsupported Responses input items return 400 (#55974), <code>browser.find</code> action type (#55305), Responses validation boundary restored (#55761), validation errors migrated to <code>VLLMValidationError</code> (#50254, #50257, #55701, #55735), unsupported <code>reasoning_effort</code> as a client error (#54022), content format detected through macro parameters (#53824), Anthropic <code>stop_sequence</code> in <code>message_delta</code> (#55325), Cohere priorities bounded (#56146), translation rejecting string files (#55618), tokenless chat and audio streams rejected (#54708), beam search respecting <code>skip_special_tokens</code> (#56211) and aborts (#56249), nested cancellation handlers removed (#55602, #57024), extension integers validated (#55606), routed-expert prompt offsets validated (#56844), MistralCommonBackend avoided for HF tokenizers (#54192), chat template fallbacks packaged (#53762), and the content format warning restored (#54622).</li>
<li><strong>Render and pooling</strong>: <code>assistant_tokens_mask</code> (#54539) and token offsets (#54692) kept aligned with truncated prompts, <code>skip_decoder_start_token</code> honored (#54799), renderer warmup bounded (#55448), LoRA adapter names in pooling responses (#56004), <code>max_embed_len</code> for chunked embeddings (#55551), body <code>request_id</code> honored (#55665), actual input usage for scoring (#56573), STEP token pooling (#55307), offline Jina token limits (#56415), and a warning for Qwen3 rerankers without a chat template (#56017).</li>
<li><strong>Rust frontend</strong>: TLS for the render server (#54999), <code>--lora-modules</code> (#54837), HTTP RL weight synchronization (#56567), HF revisions, offline mode and cache directory (#56386), <code>{% generation %}</code> chat template blocks (#56378), the <code>vllm-proto</code> crate on crates.io (#56365), <code>reasoning_tokens</code> in usage (#54883) built on token-attributed text (#52910, #54884), Mooncake/NIXL connector metrics (#52755) and <code>vllm:iteration_tokens_total</code> (#56990), gRPC preprocessed multimodal features (#55047), remote-prefill multimodal metadata (#54814) and engine errors surfaced as INTERNAL (#56405), Qwen4-exp multimodal (#54813), optional <code>--max-model-len</code> for render (#55328), per-request vocabulary counting removed (#54449), decoded chunks coalesced per update (#55012), bounded recursive parsers (#54303), tekken 0.2.0 (#53056), strict whitespace framing (#55417), consistent parser resolution (#56018), empty structured-output values rejected (#54821), selected-token logprobs (#56406), undefined token ids skipped (#55240), DeepSeek renderer fixes (#53281, #54854, #56593, #56260, #56366), and HF ShareGPT multi-turn datasets in <code>vllm bench serve</code> (#51104, #56915).</li>
<li><strong>CLI and benchmarks</strong>: <code>vllm bench serve</code> accounts for client queue time (#54136) with consistent streaming TTFT/E2E accounting (#55508), non-positive <code>--max-concurrency</code> rejected (#54887), <code>mm-processor</code> crash fixed (#56300), complete pooling responses measured (#56760), explicit empty and zero arguments honored in <code>vllm chat</code>/<code>complete</code> (#55710), <code>vllm launch render</code> shutdown hang fixed (#54913), double <code>/stop_profile</code> crash fixed (#51678), and remaining non-OpenAI content moved out of the openai folder (#56369).</li>
</ul>
<h2>Security</h2>
<ul>
<li>Validation-error response bodies bounded, closing an about 5,300x response amplification (#54684); client-supplied sparse embeddings bounded before densification (#54632).</li>
<li>Request-controlled video sampling capped for GLMGA (#54935) and Qwen-VL (#56729) backends.</li>
<li><code>cache_salt</code> validated before reaching LMCache so one request cannot take down the engine (#51444); scale-out multimodal features validated before engine handoff (#51898); late-interaction query caches keyed by server-generated ids instead of <code>X-Request-Id</code> (#51445).</li>
<li>Credentials redacted from benchmark logs (#56662); Rust frontend HTTP method labels normalized to prevent unbounded Prometheus series (#56058).</li>
</ul>
<h2>Dependencies</h2>
<ul>
<li>CUTLASS 4.7.1 (#54190) and CUTLASS DSL 4.7 (#54927), Transformers 5.16.1 (#53905), tpu-inference v0.29.0 (#56388), <code>openai &gt;= 2.25.0</code> (#49104), <code>huggingface_hub &gt;= 1.31.0</code> with httpx imported through it (#56460).</li>
<li>DeepGEMM pinned to the vllm-project fork 2.8.0 (#56876) with CUDA 12.9 release builds fixed (#57554); FlashMLA V4.1 record pin (#56893); FlashKDA bumps (#54859, #56485); FlashAttention synced with upstream (#54819); <code>_USE_MATH_DEFINES</code> defined for FlashMLA targets (#54007).</li>
<li>Public CUDA 13.4 Rubin build path and dependency pins (#54640, #56545).</li>
<li>ROCm: TheRock base on ROCm 10.0 (#55246), AITER 0.1.21.post2 (#52826, #55968), Mooncake from public wheels (#55002), AINIC libionic 54.0-187-1 pinned to a snapshot (#54112, #56459).</li>
<li>XPU: triton-xpu 3.8.0 shim (#56014), auto-round-lib 0.15.0 (#54515).</li>
<li>CPU: Docker base on Ubuntu 25.04 with GCC 15 (#49410), Zen5 image (#50314), nightly CPU images (#55163). Obsolete TPU Dockerfile removed (#55376).</li>
</ul>
<h2>Breaking Changes &amp; Deprecations</h2>
<ul>
<li>Scale-out endpoints (<code>/render</code>, <code>/derender</code>, <code>/inference/v1/generate</code>) are no longer registered on plain <code>vllm serve</code> unless <code>--enable-scale-out</code> is passed; <code>VLLM_ENABLE_SCALE_OUT_ENDPOINTS</code> was removed. <code>vllm launch render</code> and <code>vllm serve --tokens-only</code> always register them (#54579, #55176).</li>
<li>GPTQ group/dynamic activation ordering removed: <code>g_idx</code> is ignored and the related Marlin, GPTQ, CPU and RDNA3 kernels are gone (#54809).</li>
<li>Items deprecated for 0.29 removed: <code>VLLM_PREFIX_CACHE_RETENTION_INTERVAL</code> and <code>VLLM_MM_HASHER_ALGORITHM</code> env vars (use the config fields), the <code>use_fp4_indexer_cache</code> alias (use <code>indexer_kv_dtype</code>), the <code>CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES</code> fallback on ROCm (use <code>HIP_VISIBLE_DEVICES</code>), and the <code>seq_lens_cpu</code> / <code>num_computed_tokens_cpu</code> attention metadata properties (#55353).</li>
<li>The <code>all</code> Mamba cache mode is deprecated and falls back to Model Runner V1 (#55041).</li>
<li><code>python -m vllm.entrypoints.grpc_server</code> is deprecated; use <code>vllm serve --grpc</code> (#56746). <code>sse_keep_alive</code> moved to <code>vllm.entrypoints.serve.utils</code> (#56369).</li>
<li>YaRN aligned with Transformers: vendor YaRN aliases no longer multiply <code>max_position_embeddings</code> by the factor a second time, so derived <code>max_model_len</code> drops for some models (TeleChat3-36B-Thinking 131072 to 32768, sarvam-105b 5242880 to 131072); <code>attn_factor</code> and <code>extrapolation_factor</code> are ignored in favor of <code>mscale</code>/<code>attention_factor</code> (#56446).</li>
<li>Attention implementations must explicitly declare DCP support; DCP with ROCm standard attention, Triton, FlexAttention or TurboQuant now fails at backend selection (#55780).</li>
<li>MoRI-IO connector WRITE mode with hybrid KV cache groups now requires <code>--disable-hybrid-kv-cache-manager</code>; use READ mode otherwise (#53721).</li>
<li>Default audio resampler switched from PyAV to torchaudio (#52598); audio decoding is backend-selectable with soundfile first in <code>auto</code> (#51826, #55642).</li>
<li>New defaults: FlashInfer CuTeDSL NVFP4 W4A16 over Marlin on SM100/103 (#53014), W4A4 NVFP4 over weight-only kernels on SM120/121 (#55170), BF16x3 router GEMM on SM100 (#55899), DeepEP v2 combine overlap on with <code>VLLM_DEEPEP_V2_COMBINE_OVERLAP=0</code> to disable (#52781), AITER custom AG/RS on for DP attention + TP experts with <code>VLLM_ROCM_USE_AITER_CUSTOM_AR=0</code> to disable (#48247), native CUDA AttnRes for Kimi-K3 on SM100 (#54261), and <code>VLLM_USE_BREAKABLE_CUDAGRAPH=1</code> no longer overriding FULL graphs on MRV1 (#56312).</li>
</ul>

### [v0.29.0](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/releases/tag/v0.29.0)

**来源**: vLLM GitHub Releases

**摘要**: <h1>v0.29.0</h1>
<h2>Highlights</h2>
<p>This release features 594 commits from 277 contributors (91 new)!</p>
<ul>
<li><strong>Model Runner V2 is now the default for all models</strong> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/53183">#53183</a>), completing the rollout that began with pooling models (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48290">#48290</a>). MRV2 also gained CUDA graph memory profiling for KV cache auto-sizing (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/53306">#53306</a>), batch-sharded sampling that cuts per-step logits memory by 1/TP (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50465">#50465</a>), prompt embeds (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/42963">#42963</a>), <code>extract_hidden_states</code> speculation (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49811">#49811</a>), padded FULL cudagraph dispatch for uniform decode under spec decode (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/53407">#53407</a>), and DP-sync skipping before EAGLE/MTP draft prefill (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/53694">#53694</a>). MRV1 remains in use for a few ROCm models and features MRV2 does not yet support.</li>
<li><strong>New models</strong>: Hy4-preview, Tencent's 770B/49B-active MoE with Gated DeepSeek Sparse Attention and native MTP (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54160">#54160</a>); Qwen3.8-Flash-Next with BF16/FP8/NVFP4 and MTP (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/53896">#53896</a>); GraniteSWA and GraniteMoeSWA (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/52706">#52706</a>); NemotronH_Omni_Reasoning_V3 with MTP (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/52929">#52929</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/53121">#53121</a>); Kimi K3 NVFP4 checkpoints (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/53132">#53132</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Kimi-K3 and DeepSeek V4 performance</strong>: fused MXFP4 top-k finalization in the K3 latent tail (about 5% E2E latency, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/53152">#53152</a>), K3 Mamba metadata preparation in one Triton launch (6.6-7.6x kernel speedup, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/52388">#52388</a>), tuned Hopper low-latency GEMM (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54088">#54088</a>) now also dispatched on SM100 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/53534">#53534</a>) and used for <code>eh_proj</code> (12.9-25.2% kernel speedup, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/53942">#53942</a>), GEMM-RS extended to GEMM-AR (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/53053">#53053</a>), MLA gate merged into the QKV-A projection (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54015">#54015</a>), K3 DCP with DSpark (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/52188">#52188</a>) and DCP partial prefix cache hits (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50493">#50493</a>); DeepSeek V4 shared experts fused into MegaMoE (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/53040">#53040</a>), adaptive top-k width re-landed (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/52823">#52823</a>), a native SwiGLU clamp kernel for Humming MoE (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/53685">#53685</a>), and an opt-in FlashInfer <code>moe_ep</code> expert backend (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49636">#49636</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Speculative decoding</strong>: per-request acceptance stats in OpenAI API responses via <code>--per-request-spec-decode-metrics</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48915">#48915</a>), adaptive verification extended to logprobs (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/52242">#52242</a>), SM100 sparse MLA for GLM-5.2 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/52783">#52783</a>) and DeepSeek V4 on SM90 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/52795">#52795</a>), Qwen3-Omni DSpark drafts (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/52560">#52560</a>), PLaMo3 EAGLE-3/DFlash (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54239">#54239</a>), and DFlash2 loading from speculators format (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/53797">#53797</a>).</li>
<li><strong>RL weight sync</strong>: a new <code>sharded_rdt</code> P2P backend where each worker pulls only its TP/EP slice over NIXL or Ray Direct Transport (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/43375">#43375</a>), rank-local IPC weight updates (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/52497">#52497</a>), sparse checkpoint-coordinate updates through native weight loaders (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/50723">#50723</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/53751">#53751</a>), and routed expert loading for gpt-oss (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/52209">#52209</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Mamba prefix caching</strong>: internal prefill checkpoints deliver a 9%-25% TTFT improvement (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/52789">#52789</a>); <code>prefix_cache_retention_interval</code> is now a CLI argument defaulting to 0 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/52216">#52216</a>), with dense retention automatically restored for hybrid models using EAGLE/MTP (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55760">#55760</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55861">#55861</a>).</li>
<li><strong>New defaults</strong>: FlashInfer all-reduce enabled by default for TP CUDA groups, opt out with <code>VLLM_ALLREDUCE_USE_FLASHINFER=0</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/52998">#52998</a>); prefix-cache <code>NONE_HASH</code> is deterministic by default so distributed KV cache users no longer need to pin <code>PYTHONHASHSEED</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/51875">#51875</a>); new <code>--max-num-queued-reqs</code> / <code>--max-num-queued-tokens</code> admission-control flags (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49445">#49445</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Breaking changes</strong>: ten deprecated model architectures removed (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/53608">#53608</a>); FlexOlmo, Olmo3 and Hunyuan V1/VL migrated to the Transformers modeling backend (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/53615">#53615</a>); PyAV video decoder backend removed (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54231">#54231</a>); <code>python -m vllm.entrypoints.openai.api_server</code> deprecated in favor of <code>vllm serve</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/52131">#52131</a>); <code>VLLM_TEST_FORCE_FP8_MARLIN</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/52182">#52182</a>) and <code>VLLM_ROCM_USE_AITER_FP4_ASM_GEMM</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/53141">#53141</a>) removed.</li>
</ul>
<h2>⚠️ Model Runner V1 Deprecation</h2>
<p>Now that Model Runner V2 is used by default, we are considering Model Runner V1 deprecated and are targeting v0.32 for its removal. We do not intend to accept any more MRV1-specific improvements or optimizations.</p>
<p>Some <a href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/47172">features</a> are not yet supported in MRV2 but we are planning for these gaps to be closed within the next 2-3 weeks. These include sequence parallelism, dual-batch overlap, elastic expert parallellism, custom logits processors and certain speculative decoding methods. For now, vLLM will still fall back to use MRV1 if any of these features are configured.</p>
